# Seed 30 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - the first run with jitx (Iteration 109)

`seed 30 tag2 6h then experiment compare-fronts.py --rounds 10 --cpus 2,8 then
experiment evolve.py --knockout --rounds 6`, commit 09d024de2 (Iteration
108), started 13:15: four workers (cpus 2, 12, 8, 14), population 256, 95
generations before the deadline, 24,230 designs; 1,311 refused by the reach
limit, no other death. (Seed 29 never ran: the day went to seed 30.) Session
27 measured it with seeds 25-28 (calibration 1.001; cpu 2 1.001, cpu 8
1.012); the knockouts followed (results/knockouts-seed30.md).

**The stopping rule: small** - the first since seed 28.

| objective | seed 30's best | before (seeds 25-28, this session) | improvement | bar |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| fastest | 0.135 (4ddd323cee) | 0.137 (seed 28) | +1.5% | 5% |
| smallest binary + image | 24,882 (f003380307) | 24,874 (seed 28's eaa2b9e35d) | none | 2% |
| least memory | 188 KB | 188 KB | none | 8 KB |

**The front of all runs: 75** - 54 of seed 30, 21 of seed 28. The fastest
4ddd323cee 0.135 at 38,488 bytes; the smallest still eaa2b9e35d, 24,874.
Q (information): log-range best a033d2a8e1 (0.391 at 25,183 bytes, 192 KB);
plain product best 83df1cc364 (0.147 at 34,552).

**jitx**: on 1,691 of the 22,919 living, 12 of the run's 89 front designs, 8
of the 75. Its knockout (5 designs): 2.6% of speed on the selected workloads
- its 3.4x is on the held-out sieve, which selection does not see - against
an engine FILE 4.3 KB larger through the page padding (~400 bytes of code).
**t2wide** on 87 of 89, **opbody** 87, **hotinl** 46, the JIT 26, rtempty 15.

Its front born late again: 58 of 89 after generation 60 (of 95).

## The run's report

# Evolved VM designs

24230 designs evaluated, 22919 alive. Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, parse, corpus, loop,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
